兆豐銀行所做任何投資意見與市場分析結果，係依據資料製作當時情況進行判斷

市場焦點
美國
• 7月個人所得月增 0.4%、可支配所得月增 0.5%，均較前月回升，反映勞動市場與家庭收入仍
具韌性，然而個人消費支出僅月增 0.2%，實質PCE近乎持平，且儲蓄率由 6月的2.7%升至
3.0%，顯示部分新增所得轉向儲蓄 ，消費動能趨於溫和；而 7月PCE年增率維持 3.7%，核心
PCE年增3.3%，明顯高於Fed通膨目標，凸顯通膨降溫進程仍顯緩慢。整體而言，美國經濟呈
現收入成長、消費放緩及通膨黏性並存的局面，後續成長與政策走向仍將牽動市場情緒。
AI
• 輝達最新一季營收達 962億美元，創歷史新高，其中資料中心營收達 890億美元，占總營收近
93%，年增117%，為主要成長引擎 。展望第三季，公司預估營收將達 1,080億美元，季增
12%，且預測尚未納入中國資料中心運算收入 ，顯示超大規模雲端服務商及 AI業者仍持續擴

充算力。部分科技巨頭相對標普500指數的評價已降至過去十年區間相對低檔，在AI需求持續
成長及企業獲利維持強勁的背景下，後續仍具估值修復空間。
黃金
• 8月推升金價反彈的主要因素來自 ETF逢低買進與美國財政擔憂的「貶值交易」，不過上週五
聯準會新任主席華許在全球央行年會上發表鷹派立場，金價8月以來的漲幅略有收斂。
• 短線來看，聯準會基準利率的前景仍會為金價帶來波動，在較高利率水準的環境下，資金(如
ETF買盤)願意持續加碼的意願可能有所受限。待市場消化完基本面消息後，短線金價可能再度
隨著通膨及利率前景的變化而波動。

當前景氣評價改善、未來預期轉弱，消費動能面臨考驗
• 消費者信心降至 7個月低點，未來預期明顯轉弱：美國8月
諮商會消費者信心指數降至 89.4，低於市場預期，創7個月
新低；其中預期指數大幅下滑 5.8點至68.2，跌破80的歷史
警戒線，顯示消費者對未來景氣 、就業及家庭所得前景轉
趨保守。另一方面，汽油及食品價格仍偏高 ，持續影響家
庭對生活成本的感受，使短期消費動能面臨壓力。
• 現況評價改善，消費韌性未明顯轉弱：值得注意的是，8月
現況指數反向上升 6.8點至121.2，為連續三個月下滑後首
度回升；就業市場差額指標亦改善 ，顯示消費者對當前經
濟及勞動市場的評價仍具韌性 。因此，本次信心下滑較偏
向「對未來的擔憂升高」，但若預期指數持續低於 80，後
續仍須留意家庭支出與企業營收成長的潛在壓力。

• 後市觀察：消費信心轉弱，加上油價偏高、通膨黏性及就
業前景不確定，可能持續抑制非必要消費。另一方面，7月
新屋銷售下滑10.5%，顯示高利率環境仍對房市與利率敏感
型產業形成壓力。建議投資人可優先布局大型科技 、半導
體，以及受惠 AI發展與電力需求成長的公用事業及基礎建
設類股。若後續通膨持續降溫並帶動利率下行 ，則可逐步
增加消費及房地產相關類股配置。

收入增長支撐家庭財務、消費動能放緩，通膨韌性限制降息空間
• 所得增長優於消費 ，家庭支出轉趨審慎：美國7月個人
所得月增 0.4%、可支配所得 月增0.5%，均較 6月的
0.2%改善；但個人消費支出僅增 0.2%，實質PCE幾乎
持平，顯示所得成長未明顯轉化為消費動能，家庭支出
態度轉趨審慎；儲蓄率則由6月2.7%升至3.0%，反映家
庭可能將部分新增所得轉為儲蓄。
• 通膨仍具黏著性，Fed進一步寬鬆空間：7月PCE價格指
數月增 0.2%、年增 3.7%，核心 PCE月增 0.2%、年增
3.3%，明顯高於 Fed2%通膨目標；在實質消費放緩的
同時，通膨降溫速度仍不足，使Fed須在支持經濟與抑
制通膨間取得平衡，後續政策走向仍取決於通膨與就業
數據。
• 投資策略建議：通膨尚未回到目標、利率走向仍具不確

定性的環境下，短期建議採股債平衡、偏防禦配置，債
券可優先布局短中天期的美國投資級公司債，在利率仍
具不確定性下兼顧收益與利率風險；股票則可關注高股
息、現金流穩健及防禦型產業，降低對景氣循環與高估
值成長股的曝險。若後續核心PCE持續回落，才有利於
延長債券存續期間並增加成長型資產配置。

企業獲利表現強勁，科技與金融仍是市場核心動能
• 企業獲利表現強勁，科技與金融仍是市場核心動能：美股財報季進入尾聲，整體企業獲利表現優於預期，標普500成分股中
約86%企業獲利優於市場預期，整體獲利年增率達50.4%，創2021年第2季以來最佳單季表現。從產業別觀察，11大產業中
僅醫療類股獲利衰退，能源、金融及科技則為獲利預期上修幅度最大的板塊。值得注意的是，科技、金融與工業三大板塊近
三個月亦大舉推進股票回購，展現企業對自身營運的信心與企業現金流充裕，提供基本面支撐。
• 科技股宜採長期布局，定期定額降低波動影響：科技產業雖具較高波動度，但同時也是創新與長期成長動能最強的領域。對
於長期投資人而言，透過定期定額方式分散進場時點，可降低短期市場波動對投資成本的影響，並參與科技產業長期成長趨

勢。統計1971年2月至2026年7月任意期間投資那斯達克指數3年，平均報酬率接近21%，且正報酬機率超過8成，顯示面對
科技股短期波動，採取「定期定額、長期持有」策略，有助於提升投資勝率並掌握長期複利效果。
標普500指數各產業獲利成長 標普500指數各類近三個月宣布的股票回購計劃
資料來源：Factset，2026.8

顧
• AI需求持續加速，資料中心營收再創新高：輝達於8月26日盤後公布最新一季財報，營收達962億美元，創歷史新高，年增
106%、季增18%；其中資料中心營收達890億美元，占總營收近93%，年增117%，持續成為主要成長引擎。每股盈餘
（EPS）達2.22美元，獲利表現優於市場預期。展望第三季，公司預估營收將達1,080億美元，季增12%，且預測尚未納入中
國資料中心運算收入，顯示超大規模雲端服務商及AI業者仍持續擴充算力，AI需求動能並未出現明顯降溫跡象。
• AI基礎建設投資延續：輝達計畫與Apollo、BlackRock、KKR等機構募集逾5,000億美元資金，加速全球AI基礎設施建置；同
時，各國政府與跨國企業亦持續增加AI相關投資，以維持技術與產業競爭優勢。整體而言，市場先前對AI資本支出見頂的疑

慮尚未獲得基本面驗證。值得注意的是，部分科技巨頭相對標普500指數的評價已降至過去十年區間相對低檔，在AI需求持
續成長及企業獲利維持強勁的背景下，後續仍具估值修復空間。

債券比重下滑的三大原因—防禦失靈、現金崛起與股市磁吸
• 過去債券一直被譽為投資組合避風港 ，然而，彭博研究最新數據卻顯示債券 ETF佔全球ETF總資產比重已大幅下滑至約 16%，
創自2007年以來最低紀錄，相比疫情期間接近23%高峰，債券資產正在被市場邊緣化，三大原因如下：
• 股債負相關失效，減震效果不佳：過去幾十年，當股市暴跌時，債券通常會上漲，進而幫投資者減震。但數據顯示(以美國
綜合債券ETF-AGG為例)，在美股(SPY)下跌的日子裡，AGG同時上漲的機率已顯著下降。當債券無法在股市下跌時提供保
護，投資人持有傳統債券的核心理由便不復存在。
• 貨幣市場基金與超短期公債(現金類資產)強勢崛起：在高利率環境下，投資人發現持有現金比持有中長期債券更有吸引力。
貨幣市場基金與超短期公債ETF能提供與傳統債券相當甚至更好的收益率，卻低存續期間風險且流動性極佳。目前貨幣市場

基金佔基金資產比重已達24%，躍居成為市場最青睞的核心資產。
• 強勁股市與資本追求成長：在股市持續創高的牛市行情下，投資人更傾向將資金注入股票以追求高成長 (共同基金中股票佔
比高達77%創歷史新高)，或是將避險資金放在貨幣市場。
債券ETF占總資產的% AGG上漲/SPY下跌天數
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.8.28
貨幣市場基金 vs. 傳統債券基金資產

發行機構的市場轉型—被動指數退場，主動管理與靈活策略崛起
• 市場的需求直接影響金融產品供給端，ETF發行機構的動作傳遞出強烈的轉型訊號。
• 債券新產品發行量落入歷史低點：今年發行的新 ETF中，債券型僅佔約 13.8%，發行機構對推出傳統債券 ETF興趣大不如前。
• 主動型債券ETF成為主流(近60%)：在今年少數發行的債券 ETF中，近六成採用主動管理策略，而非追蹤傳統的綜合債券指
數。資產管理業者意識到，面對複雜的利率環境，投資人需要的是能夠靈活調整存續期 (久期)、精準管理信用風險並爭取超
額回報的主動型經理人，而非死板的被動追蹤指數。
• 小結及建議：在股市當前債券ETF佔比回落至2007年低點，並不意味著固定收益資產將完全消失，而是市場對債券角色進行
重新定義。傳統被動型、長存續期債券作為避險工具的效果大打折扣，資金正向極短期的現金類資產與主動靈活的債券策略

兩極化分流。針對不同需求的投資人，以下三點資產配置策略可供參考：
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.8.28
債券ETF發行趨勢1. 以現金/超短期國債替換部分傳統中長期債券：若您的目標是尋求資產組合的
穩定與流動性，建議將部分傳統債券配置移至貨幣市場基金或超短期美國國債 ，
能在鎖定高利的同時，免於承受利率升降導致的債券價格劇烈波動。
2. 放棄傳統被動債券，轉向主動型債券策略：
若仍需要固定收益來提供定期現金流，請避免盲目購買被動追蹤大盤指數的傳
統債券ETF，應優先考慮主動管理型債券ETF或基金，利用經理人的專業操作，
動態調整信用等級與存續期，以應對當前高波動的利率環境。
3. 重新評估資產組合的風險承受度：
目前許多投資人股票配置過於集中 (如共同基金股票佔比達 77%)，請檢視自身
組合，切勿因股市上漲而盲目追高；可將股票獲利落袋部分 ，靈活分配於高殖
利率超短期國債與優質主動債券型ETF或基金，建構更具韌性的投資組合。

中國政府債券融資占⽐逼近人民幣貸款
• 中國政府債券融資占⽐逼近人民幣貸款 ：過去12個月的資金流來看，中國政府債券占社會融資規模增量的⽐例自 2024年7月
起超過30%，今年7月達43%，而實體經濟發放的人⺠幣貸款占⽐為45%，這反映出融資渠道從間接融資(尤其是人⺠幣貸款)
轉向政府債券的改變，彭博預估此趨勢將會持續，因為中國官方有意在傳統銀行貸款之外轉向更依賴政府債券，並在貨幣政
策框架中，降低對貨幣供應量的重視程度，此趨勢轉變可能表示中國第四季將更依靠財政刺激來支持成長 (重點促進政府債
券需求和發行)，而⾮大幅下調政策利率或存款準備金率。
• 展望後市：第四季中國利率下行的空間有限，結構性貨幣政策工具和財政支持，仍是推動經濟成長的主要⼿段，預計中國央
行將繼續推進利率政策框架改⾰，以及債券市場國際化進程，與此同時更多政府債券的發行，以及債券估值水平的調整，可

能推動殖利率走高。

• 8月以來金價走揚：8月推升金價反彈的主要因素來自 ETF逢
低買進與美國財政擔憂的「貶值交易」，不過上週五聯準會
新任主席華許在全球央行年會上發表鷹派立場，金價8月以來
的漲幅略有收斂。

布截至8/18未償公共債務總額已正式升至 40兆美元，持有的
聯邦債務約占國內生產毛額 (GDP)的100%，逼近二次世界大
戰後的歷史紀錄，儘管隨後公布擴大公債回購規模 ，仍難以
扭轉市場對於債務問題的擔憂 ，美元信心下降、美債遭到拋
售令美元指數走弱，進而推升具資產保值特性的黃金價格。
• 觀點：除了債務問題，地緣政治風險也使美元信心下降 ，有
望為黃金價格帶來中長期的支撐 。短線來看，聯準會基準利

率的前景仍會為金價帶來波動 ，在較高利率水準的環境下，
資金(如ETF買盤)願意持續加碼的意願可能有所受限。待市場
消化完基本面消息後，短線金價可能再度隨著通膨及利率前
景的變化而波動，建議以資產配置的角度，逢回布局。 資料來源：Bloomberg，截至2026.8.27。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。

資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

2026 年 9月日銀短観予想 
AI・半導体需要で製造業は改善、非製造業はコスト増で悪化を予想

経済調査部 エコノミスト 中村 華奈子

[要約] 
 2026 年10 月1 日に公表予定の9 月日銀短観において、大企業製造業の業況判断DI（最
近）は＋25％pt（前回調査からの変化幅：＋3％pt）、同非製造業では＋34％pt（同：
▲3％pt）を予想する。 
 大企業製造業では、半導体・AI 関連需要の好調さなどを背景に、素材・加工業種とも
に景況感が改善するとみられる。 「化学」 「非鉄金属」 「電気機械」など多くの業種で業
況判断 DI（最近）の上昇を予想する。他方、 「自動車」など一部業種は低下を見込む。 
 大企業非製造業では、 幅広い業種で景況感の悪化を予想する。「小売」 「宿泊・飲食サ
ービス」などでは、仕入価格やエネルギーコストの増加などが、 「建設」 「不動産」で
は金利上昇に伴う資金調達コストの増加などが、景況感を下押ししたとみている。 
 2026 年度の設備投資計画（全規模全産業、含む土地、ソフトウェアと研究開発投資額
は含まない）は前年度比＋7.9％と、前回調査からの上方修正を予想する。省力化投資
や更新投資に加え、能力増強や半導体関連投資の需要は引き続き強く、企業の設備投
資意欲は堅調さを維持するだろう。

図表 1：業況判断 DI の予想

変化幅 変化幅 変化幅
 大企業 製造業 17　 22　 5　 17　 25　 3　 26　 1　
非製造業 36　 37　 1　 28　 34　 ▲3　 32　 ▲2　
 中小企業 製造業 7　 9　 2　 2　 10　 1　 10　 0

### Page 2

【業況判断 DI（最近） 】AI需要で製造業は改善/コスト増で非製は悪化を予想 
2026 年 10 月 1 日に公表予定の 9 月日銀短観において、大企業製造業の業況判断DI（最近）
は＋25％pt（前回調査からの変化幅：＋3％pt）、同非製造業では＋34％pt（同：▲3％pt）を
予想する（図表1）。 
製造業では、半導体・AI 関連需要の好調さを背景に、素材・加工業種ともに景況感の改善を
予想する。他方、非製造業では、中東情勢を背景としたコスト増の継続に加え、6 月の利上げ
の影響もあり資金調達コストの増加が景況感の重石となったとみている。 
大企業製造業のうち「素材業種」では、半導体・AI関連需要の拡大や価格転嫁の進展を背景
に「化学」や「非鉄金属」で業況判断 DI（最近）の上昇を見込む。 「繊維」や「紙・パルプ」
でも景況感の改善を予想する。他方、 「窯業・土石製品」ではエネルギーコストの増大が収益
を圧迫し、景況感を下押ししたとみている。 「加工業種」では、AI・半導体関連需要を受けて
「電気機械」で業況判断 DI（最近）の上昇を見込む。また「食料品」では、原材料価格や物流
費が上昇する中でも価格転嫁の進展や底堅い需要を背景に、景況感が改善したと予想する。他
方、輸出台数や国内販売台数の伸び悩みから、 「自動車」は業況判断DI（最近）の低下を見込
む。 
大企業非製造業では、 「小売」や「対個人サービス」、 「宿泊・飲食サービス」などで景況感
の悪化を予想する。中東情勢を背景とした仕入価格やエネルギーコストの増加が収益を一段と
圧迫したほか、物価高を背景とした節約志向が販売の重石となったとみている。また、 「建設」
や「不動産」でも業況判断 DI（最近）の低下を見込む。労務費や建設資材価格の上昇に加えて、
6 月の利上げの影響もあり、金利上昇に伴う資金調達コストの増加が事業環境の重石となった
とみられる。

【業況判断 DI（先行き） 】製造業の改善、非製造業の悪化は継続する見込み 
9 月日銀短観では、大企業製造業の業況判断 DI（先行き）は＋26％pt（最近からの変化幅：
＋1％pt）、同非製造業は＋32％pt（同：▲2％pt）を予想する（前掲図表 1） 。 
大企業製造業では、AI・半導体関連需要が引き続き企業の景況感を押し上げるとみている。
加工業種では「電気機械」や「はん用機械」 、 「金属製品」などで業況判断DI（先行き）の上昇
を見込むほか、 「食料品」でも価格転嫁の進展などを背景に改善を予想する。素材業種では、
「石油・石炭製品」や「窯業・土石製品」 、 「繊維」などの業種で景況感の改善を見込む。大企
業非製造業では、各種コストの高止まりや金利上昇観測 1が重石となり、幅広い業種で業況判
断 DI（先行き）が低下するとみられる。 「建設」や「不動産」で業況判断DI（先行き）の低下
を見込むほか、 「小売」や「運輸・郵便」などの業種でも、先行きの悪化を予想する。

【設備投資計画】2026 年度計画：前年度比＋7.9％と予想 
2026 年度の設備投資計画（全規模全産業、含む土地、ソフトウェアと研究開発投資額は含ま
ない）は前年度比＋7.9％と、前回調査からの上方修正を予想する（図表 3） 。人手不足対応の
ための省力化投資や更新投資に加え、能力増強や半導体関連投資の需要が引き続き強く、企業
の設備投資意欲は堅調さを維持していると見込まれる。中東情勢の悪化等に伴う原材料価格の
高騰は企業の投資判断を慎重化させ得るが、価格転嫁の進展から、コスト増による収益への影
響は限定的なものにとどまるだろう（図表4） 。

股價相對大盤走勢

聯電 (2303 TT)  Buy

中長期業績展望佳，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：展望 2026 年， AI 基礎設施的需求正帶動整體市場
復甦，並外溢至記憶體、網通與電源管理領域，然消費性電子
(如手機、 PC) 終端需求仍偏弱，且庫存天數有微幅上升的跡象。
儘管如此，聯電預期其 2026 年整體晶圓出貨量仍將成長，特別
是在 22/28 nm 與 8 吋的市占率持續提升。聯電預估 2026 年 AI
相關營收將近 3 億美元，並預期 3 年內突破 10 億美元大關。預
估聯電 2026 年稅後 EPS 7.72 元。公司在先進封裝 (interposer 或
矽電容等 )、矽光子及 Intel 合作的 12nm 陸續取得進展，為 2027
年後中長期成長奠定基礎。雖聯電 2Q26 獲利大多以投資收益
為主， 3Q26 股市成長動能相對弱，可能壓抑業外收益，但本業
獲利逐步回升，建議 Buy ，目標價 169 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.36 元：聯電 2Q26 營收為 687.33 億元，
QoQ+12.61% ，整體出貨量約當  12 吋晶圓 112.90 萬片，
QoQ+10.58% 。 2Q26 產能利用率增加至  85% 。 ASP 穩定向上。
2Q26 產能利用率增至 85% ， ASP QoQ 小增。 2Q26 毛利率增加
3.28 個百分點至 32.48% 。業外認列  302.36 億元收益，包含利
息收益  2.18 億元、匯兌收益 0.03 億元，認列投資收益  300.45 
億元 (1Q26 則為  49.97 億元投資收益 )。聯電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.36 元 。 若不計算投資收益 ，2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.97 元，QoQ+9.31% 。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元：預期 3Q26 在電腦、通訊及消費
性領域的需求動能將保持穩定。出貨成長主要由電源管理  IC 、
感測器及微控制器（ MCU ）的強勁需求所推動。 8 吋晶圓產品
線正迎來強勁反彈，預期 3Q26 產能利用率將有顯著改善；而  
12 吋產能目前已處於健康的利用率水位，足以支撐核心業務發
展。預 估 聯 電3Q26 晶圓出貨量 QoQ+7~9% ， 產能利用率約 90%。
ASP in USD QoQ+0% ，不過 2026 年的定價環境更具優勢。這主
要源於市場供需關係的結構性改善，毛利率增至約 35% 左右。
預估聯電 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元。

2 群益投顧 
  聯電 ESG ：  
聯電在 ESG 方面持續展現積極作為，並獲國內外多項評鑑肯定：  
永續策略與治理： ESG 由董事會與二位共同總經理組成的指導委員會全盤
規劃，持續以 「以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮」 為願景推動永續。 ESG
績效結合員工分紅與限制型股票條件，提升全員參與動能與企業凝聚力。  
環境面（ E）  
2025 年獲國際永續評鑑（如 FTSE Russell ）評為全台 ESG 表現頂尖企業，
半導體業唯一入選前 3。  
持續推動用水回收、能源效率提升，製程用水回收率在各廠均高於法規標
準。  
積極採購並投資離岸風電，與 CIP 渢妙風場簽 30 年綠電採購合約，展現對
2050 淨零承諾。  
社會面（ S）  
深化產學合作，與台灣 4 所大學半導體專班合作， 600 多位學生參與。  
啟動員工持股信託，落實多元薪酬政策，提升員工福祉並吸引人才。  
長期投入社會公益，如偏鄉關懷、青年培力、綠色創新等持續進行。  
公司治理（ G）  
董事會組成高度多元且獨立，獨董占三分之二、女性董事占三分之一。  
強化利害關係人溝通與資訊透明度，持續強化供應鏈 ESG 要求，並納入評
核與管理系統，推動價值鏈共榮。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 066: 260902_daiwa_NYPCB

Source PDF: all report pdf/260902_daiwa_NYPCB.pdf

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We hosted an online conference call for NYPCB on 2 
September, after trading hours. Overall, we believe the price hike trend is 
largely on track with our estimates despite a short hiccup in 4Q26E (for 
more details, please refer to our earlier note, Escalating price hike in 
substrates). Looking ahead, we believe the trend towards enlarging body 
sizes and multiple core layers in ABF substrates and the supply-demand 
imbalance in BT substrates will be key growth drivers for NYPCB.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. We are comfortable with our 
revenue growth estimate of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26E, given ongoing price 
hikes and a rising utilisation rate at Kunshan (likely to reach 100% in 
4Q26E). We believe the yield rate at Kunshan still has room to improve, 
which could negatively impact the gross margin (GM) performance in 
3Q26E. That said, compared with July, we expect the GM to improve 6-7pp 
MoM in August given the rising yield rate, and remain confident of a c.30% 
GM in 3Q26E. We expect NYPCB to achieve higher price hikes than other 
substrate vendors, as it does not sign long-term agreements with end 
customers. However, NYPCB has stated that it currently has no intention to 
hike BT substrate prices further from 4Q26, as raw material price hikes 
have eased recently; as such, we have accounted for it in our model. 
Lastly, NYPCB mentioned that it has received new AI HDI orders from a 
US-based ODM, which will result in its PCB segment reaching c.30% QoQ 
revenue growth and operating profit from 4Q26E.

Long-term investment thesis. We believe a meaningful spec upgrade is 
also likely to occur in ABF substrates, as core layer counts will need to 
increase their rigidity to handle the increasing body size of substrates. This 
trend would digest more ABF capacity and relevant raw materials (eg, T-
glass) than just unit growth from 2028E. Also, we believe this would make 
the current upcycle last longer than previous ones. As for BT substrates, 
we believe ASP growth will accelerate again as two key players are 
gradually reducing their BT capacities. Thus, we maintain our BT ASP 
assumption for 2027E despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E.

What we recommend: While we lower the 2026E EPS by 4% on a 
reduced BT price hike in 4Q26E, we keep the 2027-28E EPS largely 
unchanged. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12-month TP to 
TWD2,460 (from TWD2,444), based on an unchanged target PER of 49x 
applied to the 1-year forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-41% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on ABF/BT ASP hikes.

2 September 2026

Spec upgrade to drive additional ABF substrate demand

➢ Near-term outlook largely on track with our estimates 
➢ We believe structural changes should extend the current up-cycle  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; raising 12-month TP to TWD2,460

Company profile 
Nan Ya PCB originated as one of the business divisions of Nan Ya Plastics Corporation before 
transforming into a subsidiary in 1997, specialising in the manufacturing of printed circuit boards (PCBs) 
and integrated circuit (IC) substrates. The company’s primary end applications include networking 
(c.50%), PCs (c.20%), consumer electronics (c.10%), automotive (c.10%) and other sectors. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China.

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Ya PCB's board of directors (BoD) is responsible for overseeing the company's operations
and ensuring adherence to corporate governance principles. The board comprises individuals
with diverse expertise, contributing to effective decision-making and strategic planning. The
company emphasizes the importance of corporate governance as a set of processes, customs,
policies, laws, and institutions affecting the way the corporation is directed, administered, or
controlled. Principal stakeholders include stockholders, management, and the board of directors,
among others.
Capital management 2
Nan Ya PCB maintains a prudent approach to capital management, ensuring financial stability
and the availability of resources necessary for operational and strategic initiatives. The
company's financial statements reflect a solid capital structure, supporting its growth and
sustainability objectives.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to strict policies regarding related party transactions to maintain
transparency and fairness. Such transactions are conducted under terms comparable to those
with independent third parties and are disclosed in financial reports to ensure compliance with
regulatory standards and corporate governance best practices. For example, the company's
consolidated financial statements provide details on transactions with related parties, ensuring
transparency and accountability.
S Supply chain
management 1
Nan Ya PCB is committed to sustainable supply chain management, emphasizing environmental
protection, procurement policies, and social responsibility. The company has established a
comprehensive procurement policy that integrates sustainability considerations, ensuring that
suppliers adhere to ethical standards and environmental regulations. This commitment is part of
the company's broader sustainable responsibility policy, which includes areas such as corporate
governance, environmental protection, and social welfare.
E Data security 1
While specific details on Nan Ya PCB's data security measures are not extensively disclosed in
the available sources, the company recognizes the importance of information security in its
operations. As part of its corporate governance framework, Nan Ya PCB is likely to implement
standard data protection practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance
with relevant regulations. Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official
communications or contact the company directly for detailed information on data security
policies.

AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -7% MoM/+43%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• August sales came in at NT$3,708mn (-7% MoM/+43% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to seasonality.
• By segment, shipments of textiles (+47% YoY), electronics (+36% YoY),
machine tool (+35% YoY) and battery (+30% YoY) outperformed during the
month.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our estimate at NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41%

YoY) and 70% of consensus' at NT$11,015mn (-9% QoQ/+34% YoY).
Outlook:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue  tracking in line with our estimate.
• Management remains positive on its growth outlook driven by its share gains
and the policy-supported industry upcycle, which it expects to extend into
2027.
• Stay OW, as current valuation of 20x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its mid-
cycle average of 25x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

3324.TT 雙鴻 
 水冷板重回 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 供應鏈

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，主要生產散熱器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，
應用於筆記型電腦 、 桌上型電腦 、 伺服器 、 投影機等領域 ， 主要終端客戶為Apple(AAPL 
US)、Dell(DELL US)、Samsung(005930 KS)、HP(HPQ US)、 宏碁(2353)、 華碩(2357)
等。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,620 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，1,260 
【大盤指數/股價】47106 / 1340

➢ 2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫  
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%， 營收月減是受客
戶拉貨排程影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致。雙鴻 1H26 伺服器營收占比
已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替期，整體
2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持
全年營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在
4Q26~1Q27 明顯推升雙鴻業績 ， 且ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入 ，
此部分效益可挹注到 2027 年；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300
為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin cold plate， 已回到NVIDIA 推薦名單 ，
預期 4Q26 小量出貨；3)其他新品方面，DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26
小量出貨。CPU 均熱片 2026 年營收占比為個位數，要到 2027~2028
年效益才會較明顯。 
➢ 2027 年泰國持續擴產 
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國，泰國廠為
近年擴產重點 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、
manifold、Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/
月、manifold 3k/ 月；2)泰國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產
cold plate 、manifold，並預留電源水冷產能，若各產品線產能順利拉
升 ， 未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍 ；3)泰國三期規劃在 4Q26 動工 、2H27
投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工。 
➢ 建議調升至買進，目標價 1,620 元  
雙鴻 2H26 營運展望正向，AI 伺服器的強勢增長，能彌補消費性電子市
況低迷帶來的不利影響。 3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量，並且
cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量
出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE
區間為 8X~36X， 考量新品可逐步帶動2H26~1H27 之業績 ， 且目前PE
評價偏低，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,620 元(2027F PE 20X)。

個股報告

2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫 
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%，營收月減是受客戶拉貨排程
影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致(7 月營收包含 6 月客戶盤點遞延拉貨的 3 億元)。雙鴻
1H26 伺服器營收占比已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替
期，整體 2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持全年
營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世 ，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在 4Q26~1Q27 明
顯推升雙鴻業績，且 ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入，此部分效益可挹注到 2027
年。雙鴻估計 2026 年底 ASIC 客戶將占單月營收達 40%，2027 年 ASIC 營收占比將
擴增至 50%；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300 為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin 
cold plate ，已回到 NVIDIA 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量出貨，如此一來公司能提供
涵蓋 cold plate 、Rack Manifold 、Inner Manifold 的完整方案；3)其他新品方面，
DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26 小量出貨。CPU 均熱片(Heat Spreader)2026 年營收占
比為個位數，要到 2027 年~2028 年效益才會較明顯。 
投研部預期，在新舊平台完成交接，新品順利放量的情境下，雙鴻伺服器營收占比將
上升至 80%以上，受惠新品效應與水冷的導入，可帶動毛利率回升，投研部預估雙鴻
3Q26 營收 101.9 億元，QoQ+17.2%，YoY+71.1%，毛利率 29.8%，EPS 達 15.40
元。 
圖 1：雙鴻產品組合 1Q25~2Q26

資料來源：雙鴻 
2027 年泰國持續擴產   
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國， 泰國廠為近年擴產重點 。
各廠區規劃如下 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、manifold、
Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/月、manifold 3k/月；2)泰
國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產 cold plate、manifold，並預留電源水冷
產能，若各產品線產能順利拉升，未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍； 3)泰國三期規劃在
4Q26 動工、2H27 投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工；5)中國廠區負責生產筆電、
高階顯示卡 、 伺服器散熱相關產品 ， 整體上 ， 以氣冷模組較多 ， 也有少量的cold plate、
manifold；6)台灣主力生產 CDU (Cooling Distribution Unit)，並負責高階與高精度
液冷產品的研發和樣品試產。

不利影響 。3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量 ， 並且cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA 
Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有
利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE 區間為 8X~36X，考量新品可逐步帶動 2H26~1H27 之業

公司簡介 
台特化為半導體特殊氣體及半導體化學材
料專業開發製造商：台特化成立於 2013
年 ， 中美晶於2018 年入股 ， 持股30.93%。
公司總部及生產工廠位於彰濱工業區 ， 產品
自 製 比 例 高 ，自 製 產 品 包 含 矽 乙 烷
（Disilane） 、矽丙烷（Trisilane） 、無水氟
化 氫 （AHF ） ， 代 理 產 品 則 包 含 矽 甲 烷
（Silane） 、 其餘海外代理產品 。2Q26 特氣
營收比重約 36%，清潔營收比重約 42%，
表面精密鍍膜（Coating）及新品營收比重
約 19%。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 bill.yeh@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

台特化 (4772 TT) 
特氣、弘潔啟動擴產，營運展望正向

永豐觀點 
台特化受惠先進製程演進及擴充，對特殊氣體、清洗、Coating 需求量提高，啟動
產能擴充計劃，長期營運展望正向，研究部正向看待台特化後市獲利表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持買進 ：隨著半導體市場規模持續擴大，台特化特殊氣體、清洗、
Coating 需求量有望持續提升 ， 公司啟動擴產計劃， 預期將於4Q26 起陸續投產貢
獻，挹 注未來成長動 能， 研究部看好公司未來 產品 需求將穩定成長，預估
2026/2027 EPS 6.15/8.13 元 ， 給予歷史本益比區間中值40X，依台特化 2027 EPS
估算目標價 325 元，維持買進投資建議。

### Page 2

2

台特化為半導體特殊氣體及半導體化學材料專業開發製造商：台特化成立於 2013
年，中美晶於2018 年入股，持股30.93%。公司總部及生產工廠位於彰濱工業區，
產品自製比例高，自製產品包含矽乙烷（Disilane）、矽丙烷（Trisilane） 、無水氟
化氫（AHF） ，代理產品則包含矽甲烷（Silane） 、其餘海外代理產品。公司 2Q26
特氣營收比重約 36%，清潔營收比重約 42%，表面精密鍍膜（Coating）及新品
營收比重約 19%。 
2Q26 獲利低於預期：台特化 2Q26 營收 8.9 億元， （+4.3％ QoQ，+240.0％ 
YoY） ，低於預期，主因特殊氣體營收成長幅度未如預期，毛利率51.91%，（+0.95 
pts. QoQ，-6.41 pts. YoY） ，低於預期，主因特殊氣體營收比例較預期低，產品組
合較預期差，且因弘潔擴廠致使前期人力成本增加，影響毛利率表現所致，稅後淨
利 2.2 億元（+12.8% QoQ，+89.7％ YoY） ，稅後EPS 1.5 元，低於預期。 
台特化計畫擴產，推升未來營運動能：台特化目前特氣稼動率已達 9 成，公司啟動
擴產計畫， 矽乙烷年產能將從原先30 噸提升至 40 噸 ， 預計將於4Q26 開始動工，
預期 2Q27 完工，並於 3Q27 投產貢獻，因屬於原有產線的擴充，將不須再進行
客戶認證；AHF 增設一條產線，年產能從 10 噸提升至 20 噸，預計 1Q27 開始動
工，預期2Q27-3Q27 完工，並於4Q27 投產貢獻。因應特氣需求成長，公司亦啟
動鋼瓶容器擴增採購計畫，預期將於 2Q27 開始進行首批交付，並於 4Q27 全數
交付完畢。 
弘潔各廠區別營收佔比為中國 30%、新竹廠 50%、台南廠 20%。因客戶需求強
勁，弘潔產能已達滿載，啟動新竹廠的新廠建置及台南廠產線擴產計畫。新竹廠新
廠預計將於 1Q27 進行產線測試，預期 2Q27 正式量產營運，預計新竹廠超高潔
淨清潔產能將翻倍；台南廠超高潔淨清潔產能擴充計畫則目前正在進行客戶驗證
中，產能提升約 33%，預期將於 4Q26 驗證通過並導入量產貢獻營收。而公司表
面精密鍍膜需求同樣強勁，公司於新竹廠新廠、台南廠新產線皆有擴充計畫，預期
皆將於 2Q27 完工投產，屆時表面精密鍍膜產能將提升至目前產能的250%。隨著
台特化、弘潔產能持續擴充，將持續挹注台特化未來營運成長動能。 
產品需求穩定成長，維持買進投資建議：展望後市，隨著半導體製程演進及產能擴
充，市場規模擴大，對於特殊氣體、清洗、Coating 需求量預期將持續提升，AHF
預期也將持續放量貢獻營收，帶動獲利表現成長。短期因受限於弘潔產能已滿載，
預期弘潔營收成長幅度將趨緩 ， 抑制短期營收增幅， 然隨著公司各項擴建案陸續完
工投產，將有利提升營收及獲利表現。綜合以上，研究部看好台特化未來產品需求

營運現況與分析

將穩定成長，預估 2026 年營收 37.3 億（+87.3％YoY） ，毛利率51.99%，稅後淨
利 9.1 億元 （+46.6％YoY） ，稅後EPS 6.15 元，預估2027 年營收 46.8 億（+25.6
％YoY） ，毛利率53.19%，稅後淨利 12 億元（+32.1％YoY） ，稅後EPS 8.13 元，
參考台特化過往本益比區間位於 30-50 倍，給予歷史區間中值 40X，目標價下調
至 325 元，維持投資評等買進。

## Document 206: CTCP Tập đoàn Hòa Phát - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Tập đoàn Hòa Phát - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-07).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

KQKD 6M2026 tăng trưởng khả quan 
so với cùng kỳ

HRC là động lực chính thúc đẩy tăng 
trưởng sản lượng tiêu thụ thép

Kỳ vọng giá bán thép có xu hướng 
được điều chỉnh tăng sau Q3/2026

Tiếp tục nâng cao khả năng sản xuất 
để nắm bắt các cơ hội mới

Khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục tiêu 
32,150 VND/cổ phiếu 
 HPG ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST (loại trừ thu nhập từ chuyển nhượng dự án) 
6M2026 đạt lần lượt 108,060/11,538 tỷ VND, tăng 47%/52% svck. Động lực tăng 
trưởng chủ yếu đến từ: (1) tiến độ giải ngân đầu tư công được đẩy mạnh, (2) các dự 
án hạ tầng quy mô lớn bước vào giai đoạn thi công, (3) các biện pháp thuế quan 
giúp thu hẹp chênh lệch giá giữa HRC nội địa và nhập khẩu.

Chúng tôi dự báo tổng sản lượng tiêu thụ thép năm 2026/2027 đạt lần lượt 
14.2/15.5 triệu tấn. Lũy kế 6M2026, sản lượng bán hàng của Hòa Phát đạt 6.8 triệu 
tấn, trong đó sản lượng HRC đạt 3.4 triệu tấn tăng 57% svck, nhờ Dung Quất 2 đã 
vận hành khoảng 92% công suất thiết kế và mảng thép xây dựng tăng 17% svck 
nhưng các dây chuyền hiện tại đã hoạt động hết công suất.

Do xung đột chính trị và thắt chặt nguồn cung than cốc, các doanh nghiệp trong 
ngành đã điều chỉnh giá bán thép xây dựng và HRC bình quân lần lượt +8%/+12% 
để giảm áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận. KBSV kỳ vọng giá thép tăng trở lại từ Q4/2026 khi 
các doanh nghiệp trong ngành tăng cường nhập hàng để phục vụ nhu cầu xây dựng 
và tiến độ thi công đẩy nhanh vào giai đoạn cuối năm.

Hòa Phát đang triển khai đầu tư các dự án lớn, trong đó bao gồm (1) các hạng mục 
thuộc Khu liên hợp Dung Quất, (2) dự án nhà máy thép ray và thép đặc biệt công 
suất 700,000 tấn/năm, và (3) dự án container. Chúng tôi giữ quan điểm tích cực về 
triển vọng mở rộng danh mục sản phẩm giá trị gia tăng của HPG, song vẫn cần theo 
dõi thêm tiến độ vận hành, sản lượng thực tế và năng lực cạnh tranh được phân 
tích dưới báo cáo.

KBSV duy trì khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 32,150 VND/cổ phiếu, tương ứng 
mức lợi nhuận kỳ vọng 48.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 04/09/2026.

### Page 2

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2Q2026 Hoạt động kinh doanh

HPG (Tập đoàn Hòa Phát) là doanh nghiệp 
công nghiệp đa ngành, trong đó sản xuất và 
kinh doanh thép là lĩnh vực cốt lõi, đóng góp 
trên 97% doanh thu và 68% lợi nhuận. Tập 
đoàn cung cấp đa dạng sản phẩm như thép 
xây dựng, thép cuộn cán nóng (HRC), phôi 
thép, ống thép và tôn mạ. HPG hiện là nhà 
sản xuất thép hàng đầu Việt Nam và Đông 
Nam Á, riêng trong nước, doanh nghiệp giữ vị 
trí số một về thép xây dựng và gang thép.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Nhu cầu thép nội địa sẽ là động lực tăng trưởng chính nhờ (1) Việt Nam đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công 
và triển khai các d ự án hạ tầng trọng điểm, (2) nguồn cung bất động sản dân dụng cải thiện, (3) HRC 
nội địa sẽ thay thế HRC nhập khẩu trong dài hạn. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận gộp năm 2026 đạt 16.8% (+113 bps svck) nhờ (1) giá bán thép tăng nhanh hơn 
giá nguyên vật liệu đầu vào và (2) giá nguyên liệu đầu vào hạ nhiệt về vùng trước xung đột  
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

### Page 3

Hoạt động Kinh doanh 
KQKD 6M2026 tăng trưởng khả 
quan so với cùng kỳ

Trong 6M2026, doanh thu thuần HPG đạt 108,060 tỷ VND (tăng 47% svck). 
Trong đó doanh thu mảng thép đạt 104,275 tỷ VND (tăng 51% svck), đóng góp 
hơn 96% tổng doanh thu, trong khi doanh thu mảng Nông nghiệp thu hẹp còn 
3,286 tỷ VND (giảm 22% svck) do giá bán suy giảm. Biên lợi nhuận gộp cải thiện 
lên 17.4% (+111 bps svck) nhờ tốc độ tăng giá bán vượt tốc độ tăng chi phí đầu 
vào. LNST 6M2026 đạt 15,480 tỷ VND (tăng 103% svck), tuy nhiên nếu loại trừ 
khoản lợi nhuận bất thường từ chuyển nhượng dự án BĐS trong 1Q2026, LNST 
cốt lõi đạt 11,538 tỷ VND (tăng 52% svck).

### Page 4

Các dự án hạ tầng trọng điểm sẽ tạo 
ra nhu cầu lớn đối với các sản phẩm 
thép 
 Về mặt hành chính, Chính phủ đã thay đổi nguyên tắc để đẩy nhanh tiến độ giải 
ngân, thông qua (1) đẩy mạnh phân cấp, phân quyền cho các bộ, ngành và địa 
phương, rút ngắn các tầng phê duyệt và thời gian xử lý thủ tục, (2) đơn giản hóa 
quy trình thanh toán, điều chỉnh kế hoạch vốn, cho phép điều chuyển vốn từ 
các dự án chậm sang các dự án có khả năng giải ngân tốt hơn, và (3) áp dụng 
theo dõi, chấm điểm tiến độ giải ngân định kỳ và gắn kết quả giải ngân với 
trách nhiệm của người đứng đầu.

Theo ước tính, nhóm dự án hạ tầng trọng điểm giai đoạn 2026–2030 có thể cần 
tới 7–11 triệu tấn thép các loại. Trong đó, đường sắt sẽ yêu cầu loại thép đặc 
biệt, trong khi các dự án hạ tầng khác như cao tốc, sân bay và logistics chủ yếu 
sử dụng thép xây dựng, thép cán với nhu cầu ổn định hơn,...KBSV kỳ vọng HPG 
sẽ là doanh nghiệp hưởng lợi trực tiếp nhờ (1) năng lực sản xuất đứng đầu toàn 
ngành (2) khả năng cạnh tranh với các sản phẩm thép nhập khẩu (3) Dự án nhà 
máy thép ray và chất lượng cao sớm đi vào hoạt động.

Bảng 3: Một số các dự án hạ tầng trọng điểm 
Dự án Tổng mức đầu tư 
(nghìn tỷ đồng) 
Thời gian triển khai 
(năm) Sản phẩm thép tiêu thụ chính Nhu cầu thép ước 
tính (triệu tấn)

Nguồn: Bộ Xây dựng, KBSV

Biểu đồ 4. Tỷ lệ giải ngân 7T/2026 so với cùng kỳ  Biểu đồ 5. Sản lượng thép nội địa

Nguồn: Bộ Tài chính, KBSV   Nguồn: GSO, KBSV

Nguồn cung phục hồi ở mảng bất 
động sản dân dụng, tạo thêm động 
lực vật liệu xây dựng 
 Thanh khoản thị trường BĐS trong Q2/2026 chỉ đạt khoảng 100 nghìn giao 
dịch, giảm 28.5% qoq, nhưng lượng hàng tồn kho tăng lên 39.3 nghìn sản 
phẩm. Diễn biến trên chủ yếu do (1) quá trình tháo gỡ pháp lý thúc đẩy nguồn 
cung mới, với 113 dự án nhà ở thương mại, tương đương 103,205 căn được cấp 
phép trong Q2/2026, tăng 94.8% qoq và hơn 3 lần svck, (2) lãi suất vay mua nhà 
duy trì ở mức cao làm suy giảm khả năng chi trả và hạn chế nhu cầu đầu tư sử 
dụng đòn bẩy, và (3) cơ cấu nguồn cung vẫn tập trung ở phân khúc trung – cao 
cấp, trong khi nhu cầu ở thực tại phân khúc trung bình chưa được đáp ứng 
tương xứng, khiến tỷ lệ hấp thụ nguồn cung mới suy giảm.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng thị trường BĐS dân cư trong giai đoạn 2026–2027 sẽ cải thiện 
về cả thanh khoản và khối lượng. Nguồn cung căn hộ mới tại Hà Nội và TP.HCM

### Page 5

dự kiến lần lượt đạt khoảng 42.8/44.0 nghìn căn trong 2026/2027, tăng 8%/2% 
svck, trong khi khoảng 270 dự án nhà ở xã hội với gần 269.4 nghìn căn đang 
được xây dựng sẽ kích thích nhu cầu tiêu thụ thép thanh và thép cuộn xây 
dựng. Việc nhu cầu gia tăng cũng khiến cho các doanh nghiệp vật liệu như Hòa 
Phát, Vnsteel,... có khả năng chuyển phần tăng của chi phí nguyên liệu vào giá 
bán. Đặc biệt, Hòa Phát đang tăng trưởng nhanh hơn giai đoạn trước nhờ (1) 
duy trì chiến lược sản phẩm chất lượng cao với giá bán cạnh tranh, (2) đẩy 
mạnh góp vốn, tham gia dự án với các dự án lớn để thúc đẩy đơn hàng qua 
kênh dự án.

Nguồn: Tổng cục thống kê, KBSV   Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV

Các biện pháp phòng vệ thương 
mại gia tăng khả năng cạnh tranh 
của HRC nội địa

Áp lực giảm giá thép HRC và thép 
xây dựng nội địa đang có tín hiệu 
hồi phục

Từ 28/07/2026, Bộ Công Thương chính thức áp dụng biện pháp chống lẩn tránh 
27.83% đối với HRC có chiều rộng trên 1,880 mm đến 2,300 mm, qua đó hạn 
chế lợi thế giá của HRC Trung Quốc và thay thế hàng nhập khẩu bằng nguồn 
cung trong nước. KBSV ước tính thị phần HRC của Hòa Phát trong 6M2026 đạt 
63.6% và sản lượng nhập khẩu HRC từ Trung Quốc xuống còn 1.6 triệu tấn giảm 
24% svck.

Giá chào HRC nhập khẩu từ Ấn Độ/Indonesia hiện đã tăng lên khoảng 534–535 
USD/tấn CFR Việt Nam, trong khi giá HRC Hòa Phát duy trì quanh 14.06–14.09 
triệu VND/tấn sau khi đã giảm hơn 10% từ tháng 5. Do đó chênh lệch giá giữa 
hàng nội địa và nhập khẩu thu hẹp, giảm áp lực cạnh tranh về giá đối với các 
nhà sản xuất trong nước. Đồng thời, một số doanh nghiệp thép đã thông báo 
tăng giá 100–200 VND/kg từ đầu tháng 9 cũng cho thấy mặt bằng giá thép đang 
dần ổn định sau giai đoạn điều chỉnh trước đó. Ngoài ra, Hòa Phát thường điều 
chỉnh giá bán không đồng pha với giá nguyên liệu do: (1) độ trễ hàng tồn kho 
do chiến lược tích trữ nguyên liệu, (2) xu hướng tăng giá sớm để bảo vệ biên lợi 
nhuận, và (3) khả năng giữ giá khi cung – cầu thị trường thuận lợi.

### Page 6

Xu hướng thay thế HRC nhập khẩu 
bằng nguồn cung nội địa được kỳ 
vọng tiếp diễn trong trung – dài hạn 
 Trong ngắn hạn, nhu cầu HRC vẫn tương đối thận trọng do các doanh nghiệp 
tôn mạ, ống thép duy trì tồn kho thấp và chủ yếu mua theo đơn hàng thực tế, 
chúng tôi kỳ vọng sức cầu cải thiện từ Q4/2026 khi bước vào mùa xây dựng cao 
điểm, giải ngân đầu tư công được đẩy nhanh và tồn kho hạ nguồn dần được 
giải phóng.

Trong trung – dài hạn, KBSV kỳ vọng HRC sản xuất trong nước sẽ tiếp tục thay 
thế hàng nhập khẩu nhờ: (1) thị trường bất động sản Trung Quốc dần ổn định 
và tạo đáy, giảm áp lực xuất khẩu HRC giá thấp sang khu vực, (2) việc sử dụng 
HRC nội địa có lợi thế về thời gian giao hàng ngắn hơn, giảm rủi ro tỷ giá, đồng 
thời giúp các doanh nghiệp hạ nguồn tối ưu mức tồn kho, và (3) các doanh 
nghiệp tôn mạ, ống thép có tỷ trọng xuất khẩu cao sang Mỹ, EU... có yêu cầu 
ngày càng chặt chẽ về xuất xứ và truy xuất nguồn gốc, đáp ứng yêu cầu về quy 
tắc xuất xứ và truy xuất nguồn gốc, qua đó giảm rủi ro bị điều tra chống lẩn 
tránh biện pháp phòng vệ thương mại hoặc chuyển tải bất hợp pháp.

Biểu đồ 10. Giá HRC nhập khẩu từ Trung Quốc và Ấn Độ  Biểu đồ 11. Sản lượng HRC của Hòa Phát

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV   Nguồn: HPG, KBSV

HPG sẽ cho ra sản phẩm đầu tiên từ 
Q2/2027 nhưng dự kiến đóng góp 
đáng kể vào hoạt động kinh doanh 
từ 2028 
 Nhà máy ray và thép đặc biệt của HPG đã hoàn thành trên 50% khối lượng xây 
dựng và dự kiến cung cấp sản phẩm thương mại từ Q2/2027. Trong khi đó, 
VinMetal cũng tham gia phân khúc thép ray, dự kiến vận hành thương mại từ 
Q1/2031, chủ yếu phục vụ nhu cầu trong hệ sinh thái Vingroup. Dự án VinMetal 
Hà Tĩnh hợp tác với Pomina có tổng mức đầu tư gần 80,000 tỷ đồng, công suất 
thiết kế khoảng 5 triệu tấn thép/năm, trong đó công suất HRC tối đa khoảng 3 
triệu tấn/năm, tương đương khoảng 33% công suất HRC của HPG. Về năng lực 
cạnh tranh, VinMetal áp dụng quy trình luyện thép tích hợp lò cao – lò thổi ôxy 
(BF–BOF), tương đồng với công nghệ của HPG. Tuy nhiên, trong giai đoạn đầu 
VinMetal có thể cần thời gian để nâng công suất, ổn định định mức tiêu hao 
nhiên liệu và tối ưu hiệu suất thiết bị, khoảng 42% nhu cầu điện dự kiến huy 
động từ lưới điện quốc gia.

Nguồn: HPG, KBSV

Khuyến nghị MUA với HPG, giá mục 
tiêu 32,150 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi sử dụng 2 phương pháp định giá là chiết khấu dòng tiền doanh 
nghiệp (FCFF) và so sánh P/B lịch sử của Hòa Phát để định giá cổ phiếu HPG, 
với tỷ trọng 50% cho mỗi phương pháp. Với lợi thế dẫn đầu ngành về quy mô và 
thị phần, chúng tôi kỳ vọng HPG sẽ duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng doanh thu bình 
quân 15% CAGR trong giai đoạn 2026–2028F. Các rủi ro cần lưu ý bao gồm (1) 
giá nguyên vật liệu đầu vào tăng cao trở lại sẽ ảnh hưởng đến biên lợi nhuận 
nhiều hơn dự kiến, (2) nhu cầu thị trường suy yếu hơn dự kiến sẽ ảnh hưởng 
đến sản lượng tiêu thụ, và (3) cạnh tranh trong ngành gia tăng có thể tạo áp lực 
lên giá bán và thị phần của doanh nghiệp.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh và kết quả định giá, chúng tôi đưa ra khuyến 
nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 32,150 VND/cổ phiếu, tương ứng mức tăng giá tiềm 
năng 48.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 04/09/2026.

### Page 1

九月 02, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  硬體 ‧美國

Dell 
(DELL.O/DELL US)

AI伺服器積壓訂單季增 85%，營收展望再次優於預期 
重要訊息 
Dell FY2Q27  EPS 優於市場共識 43%，主要來自伺服器營利率優於預期， 公
司上修 FY2027 AI 伺服器營收展望約 23%，帶動盤後股價上漲約 7%。 
評論及分析  
FY2Q27 EPS 年增 203%至 7.04 美元、優於市場共識 43%。Dell FY2Q27 
(2026/05- 2026/07)營收年增 58%、季增 7%至 470 億美元，主要受惠: (1) 
AI 伺服器營收年增 100%、季增 2%至 164 億美元，GB 出貨櫃數約 4,000
櫃，且 AI 伺服器積壓訂單季增約 85%至 950 億美元；(2) 通用型伺服器與
網通營收年增 122%、季增 23%至 105 億美元，主要受惠 AI 推理需求、公
司調漲報價以轉嫁記憶體成本，再加上低價記憶體庫存貢獻，推升整體 ISG
業務營利率年增 6.2ppts、季增 4.5ppts 至 15.0%，帶動 FY2Q27 EPS 年增
203%、季增 45%至 7.04 美元，優於凱基與市場共識預估 38%/43%。 
Dell 上調 FY2027 AI 營收至年增 200%、EPS 年增 148%。公司提及伺服
器高營利率不具延續性，預期 FY3Q27 回落至 13-14%、約當年增 1ppt，但
在 AI 伺服器營收展望季增約 16%至 190 億美元、整體營收展望仍優於預期
下，FY3Q27 EPS 財測仍為年增 151%、季減 8%至 6.50 美元、優於市場共
識 46%。基於 AI 伺服器訂單成長，公司將 FY2027 AI 伺服器營收展望由
600 億美元上修約 23%至 740 億美元，隱含年增約 200%，並將 EPS 財測
上修約 42%至 25.50 美元、約當年增 148%，優於市場共識 33%。 
預估 FY2027/FY2028 EPS 為 26.66/30.06 美元。基於 Dell AI 伺服器營收
展望優於凱基先前預估，且通用型伺服器營利率亦優於預期，凱基上修 Dell 
FY2027/FY2028 EPS 預估自 20.35/24.50 至 26.66 /30.06 美元。 
投資建議  
凱基維持「增加持股」評等，目標價 上修至 600 美元 (20x FY202 8F EPS)。 
投資風險  
AI 市況因 CSP 廠放緩資本支出而反轉 ；客戶因經濟衰退而下修訂單量 。

### Page 2

美國 Dell

九月 02, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

FY2Q27 Dell AI 伺服器營收占比為 35%。Dell 主要業務為提供電腦相關產
品，包含筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、平板電腦、工作站、伺服器、螢幕、印
表機、閘道器、軟體、儲存設備，以及網路等產品。Dell 業務可分為基礎設
施解決方案 (Infrastructure Solutions ；ISG；FY1Q27 營收占比 68%)、客戶
解決方案(Client Solut ions；CSG；32%)。其中 (1) ISG ：產品包含伺服器、
網路產品，以及儲存解決方案，其中 AI 伺服器營收 FY2Q27 占比達 35%，
GB 出貨櫃數與 FY1Q27 大致持平於 4,000 櫃。Dell 目前透過推出 AI Factory
整合平台，提供從資料準備、模型訓練到部署與管理的解決方案，透過結合
軟硬體的方式，使 Dell AI Factory 的部署成本比公有雲解決方案減少約
60%，並提升 20%-40%的生產力。 (2) CSG ：主要提供品牌個人電腦，包
含筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦與工作站，以及品牌週邊產品，如顯示器、 擴充
底座、鍵盤、滑鼠、網路攝影機與音訊裝置、第三方軟體與週邊設備；服務
類產品則包含系統配置、支援與部署服務。FY1Q27 PC 營收約 89%來自商
務 PC、11%來自消費性 PC。 
預估 Dell FY2027/FY2028 AI 伺服器營收年增 209%/60%，為營運成長主
要動能。Dell 提及目前已有超過 6,500 家企業客戶，受惠於 AI 推論需求使
中小型企業用戶的拉貨速度升溫，FY 2Q27 AI 伺服器積壓訂單季增 85%至
950 億美元，其中多數為 Vera Rubin 伺服器。基於 AI 訂單需求優於預期，
Dell 將 FY2027 AI 伺服器營收展望由 600 億美元上修約 23%至 740 億美
元，隱含年增約 200%，並隱含 FY2H27 AI 伺服器營收較 FY1H27 成長
28% 。 FY2028 的部分，公司雖未提供確切展望，但認為自身在 AI 
Factory、AI 伺服器、儲存設備與終端裝置等皆有完整布局，因此在面對競
爭者日益增加的環境下仍能持續提升供貨份額，整體營收成長性將維持在優
於產業平均的水準。基於 Dell 對 FY2027 AI 伺服器展望優於凱基先前預估，
且對 FY2028 成長性亦抱持樂觀看法，凱基 上修 Dell FY2027/FY2028 AI 伺
服器營收預估至 762/1,218 億美元，分別為年增 209%/60%；整體 ISG 業
務營收預估為 1,383/1,946 億美元，分別為年增 127%/41% 。 
PC 業務的部分， 隨記憶體低價庫存貢獻逐步降低， 公司預期營利率將逐步
回到往年區間上緣的 6%。公司表示未來幾季將持續採取將記憶體成本轉
嫁、調漲 PC 售價的策略， 目前觀察到 商務 PC 需求相對消費性仍更為穩
固，使商務 PC 營收占比超過 85%的 Dell 可望享有優於產業平均的成長動
能，凱基預估 Dell FY2027/FY2028 CSG 業務營收為 588/646 億美元，分別
為年增 15%/10%。

Dell 評價偏低，維持「增加持股」之評等，目標價 上修至 600 美元。整體
而言，基於 Dell AI 伺服器營收展望優於凱基先前預估，且通用型伺服器營
利率與展望皆優於預期，凱基將 Dell FY2027 /FY2028  EPS 預估自
20.35/24.50 上修至 26.66/30.06 美元，分別為年增 159%/13%。相較市場
共識 FY2027/FY2028 EPS 預估為 19.17/23.59 美元，目前股價交易於約
15/19 倍之 FY2028 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Dell 過往五年本益比多落於 5-20 倍之間，考量 (1) Dell 具備 AI 伺服器軟硬
體整合能力，使其為多家 企業用戶與 Neocloud 的主要供應商； (2) Dell 商務
電腦營收占比 高於多數同業 ，轉嫁記憶體漲價之能力較佳 ，並在 AI 與換機
需求下，有助公司營利率維持在往年平均 5-6%之區間上緣；(3) 隨 AI 伺服
器良率提升與規模成長 ，預期 Dell AI 伺服器營利率將逐步由約 3-4%提升至
5%，有助整體獲利性轉佳 。綜合上述原因，預期 Dell 本益比將回升至區間
上緣之 20 倍上下浮動；基於當前評價偏低，故維持「 增加持股」之評等；
基於 EPS 預估調升，故將目標價自前次給予之 495 美元上修至 600 美元 
(20x FY202 8F EPS)。

### Page 6

美國 Dell

九月 02, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  6

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案

## Document 227: FX Daily_ The hawkish bar is set high for the ECB

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ The hawkish bar is set high for the ECB.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Article | 10 September 2026 1
THINK Economic and financial analysis
Article  |  10 September 2026   FX
FX Daily: The hawkish bar is set high for
the ECB
As the ECB delivers its well-telegraphed 25bp hike, we doubt it will signal
enough commitment to further tightening to validate the market’s
aggressively hawkish pricing. That leaves EUR/USD exposed to downside
risks, particularly as the dollar may find additional support after
yesterday’s smaller-than-expected Treasury buyback announcement

Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent announced that the US would buy back $6bn worth of government
debt – but market expectations were clearly of a higher figure
USD: Some Bessent risk premium leaving the dollar?
Poor price action for the dollar persisted for most of yesterday. Then came the US Treasury
announcement that it would buy back $6bn in long-term bonds, triple the amount announced
in August. The bond market’s negative reaction signals that expectations were clearly of a

higher figure – somewhere around $10bn had been rumoured. However, that prompted a
dollar rebound, as some risk premium linked to outsized Treasury intervention was unwound.
The currency market is unlikely to shelve the debasement narrative just yet. But yesterday’s
announcement could help create a better environment for the dollar, potentially allowing it to
respond more efficiently to external drivers such as higher oil prices and weaker equities, both

### Page 2

THINK Economic and financial analysis
Article | 10 September 2026 2
USD positives.
Today, focus will be on US PPI inflation for August, which should have a higher-than-usual
market impact ahead of tomorrow’s CPI and next week’s FOMC. Consensus is for 0.4% MoM
headline PPI, and 0.3% for core. We feel these are figures that can – if anything – slightly
reinforce market conviction on a September hike: pricing is currently 16bp.
We continue to see upside risks for the dollar. If front-end USD rates remain around current

levels and global sentiment stays fragile, we cannot see a fundamental reason for persistent
USD underperformance. Unless inflation delivers material downside surprises today or
tomorrow, the conditions appear in place for DXY to return to 99.0.
Francesco Pesole
EUR: ECB can disappoint the hawks
EUR/USD climbed to 1.650 yesterday before reversing after the US Treasury announcement.
The initial rally was driven by a sizeable 7-8bp tightening in EUR/USD two-year swap

differentials, entirely the result of an almost 10bp rise in the euro leg. That likely reflected
spillover from another rise in energy prices and perhaps some positioning for a hawkish ECB
message today.
On the latter, we aren’t convinced. As discussed in our ECB cheat sheet, there are some
downside risks for the euro today. Markets now price 50bp of tightening by year-end and 85bp
by July, leaving the ECB with a high bar to meet. While today’s widely expected 25bp hike can

still be framed as an insurance move, further hikes would push policy further into restrictive
territory. We doubt the ECB is ready to semi-commit to another hike by year-end, as it did in
July, for two main reasons: a) the inflation projections are unlikely to justify it; b) concerns may
be building around the eurozone bond market.
To be clear, we don’t think the ECB will be intentionally dovish. But retaining broad optionality
on further tightening seems too little to satisfy markets’ hawkish bets. We expect some

repricing lower in the EUR curve to pave the way for a retest of 1.160 ahead of next week’s
FOMC (where we expect a hike). Our one-month target remains 1.150.
Francesco Pesole
CEE: Central banks leave room for weaker zloty and koruna
As expected, the National Bank of Poland kept rates unchanged at 3.75% yesterday. The
statement offered little new guidance, leaving attention on Governor Adam Glapinski’s press
conference today at 3pm local time. We expect a somewhat more hawkish tone than in July,

reflecting higher inflation and a firmer outlook. However, with markets pricing in around 85bp
of tightening, the bar for a hawkish surprise is high. We therefore see scope for some easing in

### Page 3

THINK Economic and financial analysis
Article | 10 September 2026 3
rate expectations and a rise in EUR/PLN, also supported by record gas prices, potentially taking
the pair back above 4.320.
In the Czech Republic, the CNB blackout period begins today. Deputy Governor Eva
Zamrazilova, one of the board’s most hawkish members, said yesterday that she favours
keeping rates unchanged at next week’s September meeting, strongly signalling the likely
outcome for the broader board. The CNB could still communicate today, but surprises appear

unlikely. Despite the ongoing rates sell-off, the interest-rate differential has narrowed and, in
line with our Monday call, EUR/CZK has moved back above 24.250. The pair is currently
consistent with the rates move in our models, although a more dovish CNB decision than
markets expect next week could push it higher.
Frantisek Taborsky
TRY: Rates on hold but cuts ahead
We expect the CBT to keep rates unchanged at 37% today. It is too early to resume easing

after the bank restarted weekly repo auctions and brought the effective funding rate down
from 40% to the policy rate. Still, weaker-than-expected 2Q GDP and a continued gradual
decline in inflation should allow two 100bp cuts to 35% in Q4.
As liquidity conditions have normalised, market pricing has turned more dovish and moved
closer to our year-end forecast. The CBT rate is now priced at 34.50% by year-end. However,
markets remain sceptical about the scope for easing next year, pricing only around 100bp of

cuts. Continued disinflation could drive further dovish repricing in this part of the curve.
The FX outlook is broadly unchanged. Long TRY positioning has already returned to pre-US-Iran
conflict levels, despite the CBT’s dovish August stance and the prospect of renewed easing. At
the same time, the continued recovery in central bank FX reserves should support investor
demand for the TRY carry trade. We forecast USD/TRY at 52 by year-end and 63 by the end of

next year.
Frantisek Taborsky

AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q2 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 53.3%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 49.5%. 
 Of the 492 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q2, 86.0% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.5% and prior four quarter average of 80%. 
 26Q2 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 15.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 13.5%. 
 77.3% of companies have reported 26Q2 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 62.8% and an average over the past four quarters of 77%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 36 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 63 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 36 by 63 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q3– 27Q2) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 20.3. 
 During the week of Sep. 7, six S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

earnings. Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 25% since the start of the quarter.

earnings, while the communication services (70B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 171.7B since the start of the quarter.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q2 is 53.3%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 49.5%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $858.9B in 26Q2, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $560.2B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q2. 
10 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q2. The Energy and 
Communication Services sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Health Care sector 
has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q2. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (142.7%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $55.8B in 26Q2, 
compared to earnings of $23B in 25Q2. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (354.1%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (166.5%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 60.1%. 
The Communication Services sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (114.2%) of any sector. It is 
expected to earn $140B in 26Q2, compared to earnings of $65.3B in 25Q2. 7 of the 9 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Cable & Satellite (397.4%) and Interactive Media & Services 
(174.3%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the 
growth rate declines to 3.5%. 
The Health Care sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (-6.5%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $65.9B in 
26Q2, compared to earnings of $70.5B in 25Q2. 2 of the 10 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see lower 
earnings than a year ago. The Biotechnology (-67.6%) and Pharmaceuticals (-9.2%) sub-industries have the lowest 
earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate improves to 22.7%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST 
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which 
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted 
average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our 
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate), 
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time. 
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.  
StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst 
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM 
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.  
Over the next two weeks, six S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 0 positive 
surprises and 0 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

## Document 268: PPI见顶后：下半场的轨迹

Source PDF: all report pdf/PPI见顶后：下半场的轨迹.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

国联⺠⽣宏观认为， 8 ⽉ PPI 同⽐阶段性回升并⾮物价下⾏趋势反转，⽽是油价反弹、 AI 产
业链⾼景⽓与基数效应共同作⽤的结果。预计 6 ⽉ 4.1% 已是年内⾼点， 9 ⽉或现阶段性 “ ⼩
⾼峰 ” ，随后四季度重回缓步回落轨道。
摘要：
8 ⽉ PPI 同⽐数据的阶段性回升，是否意味着物价已经扭转了前期的下⾏轨迹？单⽉的反弹或许
带有⼀定脉冲性质，相较之下，隐藏在⽔⾯之下的结构性切换其实更为关键：上半年，在外部
地缘博弈与供给冲击的交织下，上游⼤宗商品价格的脉冲式攀升成为了推⾼物价的主⼒；⽽进
⼊下半年，输⼊性动能虽有震荡扰动，但其⽀撑⼒度或难以复制上半年之强， AI 相关产业持续⾼
景⽓带来的价格⽀撑，正悄然接棒成为当前物价图景中的结构性亮⾊。
PPI ⾼点已过：如何在结构分化中寻找定价之锚
PPI 不仅是宏观经济感知供需冷暖的 “ 温度计 ” ，更是资本市场中预判企业盈利周期与⼤类资产定
价⾛向的关键锚点。复盘历史可以清晰地看到，当 PPI 环⽐处于持续为正的扩张期时，股市往往

能展现出较强的上⾏弹性，这为市场奠定了积极的⻉塔基调。更重要的是，上半年 AI 相关⾏业
PPI 增速的逆势⾛强，正与科创 50 指数的⾛势形成紧密的共振，⼀定程度上为结构性⻉塔的确认
提供了新的视⻆。
⾯对总量下⾏与结构分化的复杂局⾯，如何提前且精准地捕捉 PPI 的真实脉动？我们试图分别构
建近⽉拟合与远⽉映射两套量化预测模型，以期在不确定的宏观环境中，寻得研判⼤类资产⾛
势的坚实基⽯。
当前 PPI 结构：管窥经济温差的 “ 四重分化 ”
欲前瞻未来，必先解构当下。细究 2026 年以来的 PPI ⾛势，总量回升的表象下潜藏着极具张⼒的
“ 四重分化 ” ：
其⼀为 “ 供强需弱 ” ：利⽤经济学中经典的 Blanchard-Quah 结构向量⾃回归（ BQ SVAR ）模型，
将 PPI 变动分解为供给与需求两端驱动源后发现，本轮物价抬升主要由供给端约束撬动，需求侧
的⽀撑相对平缓。
其⼆为 “ 外热内温 ” ： 以中东局势为代表的外部扰动使得原油等国际定价商品率先发⼒，地缘博
弈推⾼的输⼊性成本成为上半年 PPI 攀升的主要动⼒，⽽内源性动能的接⼒则相对克制。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

其三是 “ 上热下冷 ” ：上游资源品尽享涨价红利，但成本向下传导滞缓，致使中下游企业利润空
间承压。在 “ 供强需弱 ” 的宏观背景下，价格红利向上游环节集中，下游⾏业却仍⾯临有效需求不
⾜，企业盈利修复⾯临双向挤压。
其四为 “ 新⽼交替 ” ：乘着全球 AI 产业发展的东⻛，电⼦通信等新质⽣产⼒相关产业接棒成为物价
抗跌的韧性所在，折射出经济动能加速切换的微观镜像。特别是在 5 ⽉下旬国际油价阶段性回
落、输⼊性通胀退潮之际，新产业相关价格仍保持环⽐上涨，正逐步成为 PPI 企稳的重要接续⼒
量。
近⽉预测：破局数据时滞的 “ ⾼频捕捉 ”
由于官⽅数据通常滞后于实际经济进程半个⽉以上，难免存在⼀定的信息时滞。为了在数据发
布前的真空期内提前感知 PPI 的真实脉动，我们将⽬光锁定在对总量波动起决定性作⽤、权重占
约 75% 的⽣产资料上，以此构建近⽉⾼频拟合模型。
在变量精炼环节，我们以统计局旬度公布的 50 种重要⽣产资料为基础，开辟了静态与动态两条
降维路径，以避免过度拟合。由于部分⽣产资料价格具有较强的共线性，静态筛选采⽤了

LASSO 正则化降维技术，在惩罚系数为 0.0313 时，模型在均⽅误差与维度之间取得了最佳平
衡。遵循 “ 板块内择优 ” 原则，我们先在 9 个板块内部各选出 1 个解释⼒最强的品种，随后综合板块
覆盖度进⼀步精简，最终锁定⽆缝钢管、 LPG 、⼭西优混、尿素、聚丙烯这 5 个核⼼品种。这⼀
⽅法在最⼤程度保留板块代表性的同时，也保持了较强的经济学含义可解释性。

### Page 3

动态筛选则引⼊ 24 个⽉滚动窗⼝，并配合贪⼼算法进⾏特征动态寻优，精准提取出⼭西优混、
LPG 、⽔泥、电解铜 4 ⼤品类。相⽐于静态模式，动态筛选能够持续淘汰旧信息并纳⼊新信息，
确保参数及时跟进经济环境的边际变化。
从回测效果来看，该模型在每⽉ 14 ⽇即可给出当⽉物价初窥，次⽉ 4 ⽇便能完成全⽉拼图，相较
官⽅发布⽇抢跑近⼀周。以 7 ⽉为例，两种路径给出的预测值（ 3.6% 与 3.4% ）均贴近 3.5% 的实
际公布值，展现出较为稳健的跟踪效能。
远⽉预测：破译商品传导的 “ 跨期映射 ”

### Page 4

近⽉拟合固然解决了数据的时滞痛点，但⼤类资产定价往往需要更为前瞻的视野。在直接构建
远期量化模型前，如果仅需判断物价的⼤趋势， M1 同⽐是⼀个效果较好的领先指标。从⻓时间
序列来看， M1 同⽐稳定领先 PPI 同⽐约 9 个⽉。其传导逻辑⼗分清晰： M1 上升意味着资⾦活化
程度提⾼，企业获得资⾦后扩⼤⽣产与采购，进⽽推⾼⼯业品出⼚价格。基于此，⾃ 2025 年 10
⽉以来进⼊下⾏趋势的 M1 同⽐，同样预⽰着 PPI 同⽐或将从 2026 年 7 ⽉起进⼊下⾏通道。
不过，尽管 M1 有效，但考虑到其统计⼝径近期的调整以及历史上的阶段性背离，我们仍需⽴⾜
于实体经济，从⼤宗商品价格出发构建量化预测逻辑。
⼤宗商品价格向中下游的传导并⾮⼀蹴⽽就，我们恰可利⽤这⼀天然的 “ 时间差 ” ，采取 “ 两步
⾛ ” 策略构建映射模型。⾸先是搭建跨期传输链条——PPI 涵盖 40 个⼯业⾏业，其对总量读数的
影响受⾏业权重与价格波动性的共同影响。我们以 2025 年各⾏业规上企业的营收占⽐近似作为

权重，结合 2021 年以来的波动率发现，⿊⾊、化学原料、有⾊、⽯油、煤炭五⼤⾏业是 PPI 同⽐
的主要贡献者。
我们将这五⼤⾏业代表性品种的价格同⽐滞后 1 ⾄ 3 期后进⾏测试，发现诸如甲醇、 LME 铜等品
种在滞后 2 期时与 PPI 的相关性甚⾄超越了当期，进⼀步印证了传导的滞后特征。通过 36 个⽉滚
动窗⼝外推与连续性约束，模型最终筛选出包含 12 个⾃变量的最佳预测模型（涵盖当期及滞后 1
期的螺纹钢、滞后 1-3 期的 LME 铝等）。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
其次是注⼊前瞻价格预期。回测结果显⽰，虽然模型的平均均⽅根误差（ RMSE ）随预测⽉份的
推远⽽⾃然上升，但该模型对未来 1-2 个⽉的 PPI ⽅向预测准确率仍稳定在 80% 以上。我们借助
活跃期货市场的价格发现功能，将螺纹钢、 LME 铝等远⽉期货合约价格转化为隐含价格变动代
⼊模型（缺乏活跃合约的动⼒煤则以近 3 ⽉价格环⽐均值替代），从⽽完成从历史传导规律向未
来趋势前瞻的跨越。
未来路径：峰值已过，静待⾦秋余波
站在当前时点，我们将上述测算的价格输⼊远⽉模型，最终得到了 2026 年 9 ⽉⾄ 2027 年 1 ⽉的
PPI 同⽐预测值，分别为 3.8% 、 3.5% 、 3.3% 、 3.0% 和 2.6% 。基于这⼀结果，我们为下半年 PPI
运⾏轨迹勾勒出 “ 峰值已过、三季度反弹、四季度回落 ” 的三条核⼼主线：
⾸先， 6 ⽉ PPI 同⽐ 4.1% 的读数⼤概率已探明年内⾼点。该年中峰值主要源于⼆季度地缘冲突催
化的油价急涨，在中性预期下，此类量级的单边供给冲击难以复现， 7 ⽉同⽐回落⾄ 3.5% 也初步

印证了物价中枢有所下移的态势。
其次， 9 ⽉或将迎来年内 PPI 同⽐的阶段性 “ ⼩⾼峰 ” 。这并⾮通胀周期的全⾯反转，⽽是三重⼒
量的短期交汇：⼀是 7 ⽉中旬以来地缘冲突反复带来的油价反弹，其滞后效应将在 8 ⾄ 9 ⽉集中释
放， 8 ⽉ PPI 同⽐较上⽉抬升 0.3 个百分点⾄ 3.8% 的事实已初步验证了这⼀传导；⼆是 AI 产业链的
⾼景⽓度正持续为上游有⾊⾦属提供结构性⽀撑，展现出较强价格韧性；三是基数效应⽅⾯， 9
⽉尚存 0.4 个百分点的翘尾因素发挥托底作⽤。这三重动⼒有望在季末形成共振，托举读数阶段
性上⾏。
最后，四季度起总量读数或将重新进⼊缓步回落轨道。10 ⽉以后，翘尾因素对 PPI 同⽐的⽀撑将
减弱，若后续未⻅超预期的内需强刺激政策或新的外部供给扰动接棒， PPI 同⽐读数将⾯临温和
下⾏的现实压⼒。
本⽂来源：国联⺠⽣宏观刘宏彦、钟渝梅、陶川

### Page 6

市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 302: The U.S. Corporate Tax Base That Now Resides in Ireland (2026-09-03)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The U.S. Corporate Tax Base That Now Resides in Ireland (2026-09-03).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 15
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
The U.S. Corporate Tax Base That Now Resides in
Ireland
America’s largest companies often pay more tax in Dublin than they do in D.C.
PUBLISHED
September 3, 2026 6:43 p.m.
Articles Follow the Money Share
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
Michael Weilandt
Research Associate, Geoeconomics
This is a joint post with Michael Weilandt, a
Research Associate at the Council on Foreign
Relations
Foreign multinationals, mostly American

multinationals, booked just under $300 billion in
profits in Ireland last year.
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
That isn’t just because Ireland used to have a 12.5
percent headline corporate tax rate, and its current 15
percent rate on large firms is below the U.S. headline
rate of 21 percent.
Ireland also permits deductions for qualifying
expenditure on intangible assets, including the
purchase of intra-group intellectual property, which
can substantially reduce firms effective Irish tax rate.*
And since Ireland is a member of the OECD and
most firms now want to book their profits in “BEPS”

compliant jurisdictions, Ireland is the center for
corporate tax avoidance that isn’t (quite) a pure tax
haven.
The names of the firms that take the most advantage
of lower tax rates in Ireland aren’t exactly a secret.
Apple—widely thought to be Ireland’s largest
individual taxpayer thanks to its mostly Irish $80

### Page 3

3
billion “global” profit—hasn’t reported the amount of
cash it paid the Irish government in 2025.
Microsoft has reported the tax it pays the Irish
government. Per its latest 10-K (p.75), in FY 2026, it
booked over 80 percent of its $62.5 billion
international profit in Ireland. Microsoft also reports
that it actually paid $6.5 billion to the Irish
government (and another $5.5 billion in tax in other
foreign jurisdictions), more than the $6.25 billion it

paid to the U.S. Treasury. Paid here means a transfer
to the Irish treasury, not a set aside to cover expected
FY 2026 taxes.** It set aside $12 billion to cover
expected 2026 foreign tax payments, and $2.5 billion
for expected U.S. federal income tax.
Eli Lilly, like Microsoft, paid more in tax to Dublin
than to D.C. Eli Lilly’s 10-K states that it paid $6.6
billion in tax to Ireland in 2025, and only $3.3 billion
to the U.S.
Now, Eli Lilly likely also played a bit of a tax game in

(calendar year) 2025, as its Irish tax payments were
unusually high.
Lilly and the other big pharmaceutical companies
shipped to the United States back in the first quarter
of 2025. Back then the pharmaceutical companies
were petrified that President Trump might actually
impose real tariffs on profitable American firms
playing tax games abroad. Those tariffs though never

### Page 4

4
materialized. Firms did “deals” with the Trump
administration instead.
But the threat of tariffs had a huge impact on the
trade data—Lilly rushed to produce Mounjaro’s
active ingredient in its Irish factories, and it then
rushed the product to the U.S. ahead of the tariff
deadline (there is a six-digit line item in the trade
data that is more or less Mounjaro, for the curious).
And it seems like Lilly’s Irish subsidiary booked the
profit on its sales to Lilly U.S. ahead of any actual sale

to U.S. consumers.***
The net result, obviously, was a big Irish tax liability.
And a big windfall for Ireland’s Treasury, as Lilly
displaced Pfizer as Ireland’s third largest corporate tax
revenues.
The $15 billion a year that these firms generally pay
in tax in Ireland is real money, as they say (they paid

### Page 5

5
€13 billion in 2024 according the Irish fiscal council;
the 2025 number will be out soon).
Ask the top brass at the Pentagon. $15 billion a year
is probably three new attack subs, or five Arleigh
Burke class destroyers (think mobile air defense
systems). Or for several batteries of Patriots (if
Raytheon can make them). Had those funds been
paid to the U.S., it would not quite cover the cost of
an aircraft carrier (costs have increased recently) but

it would have come close. $30 billion over two years is
a carrier task force. Enough, in other words, to make
the tradeoff between guns and butter a bit less acute.
That is simply from tax that firms are paying in
Ireland on the profit from intellectual property largely
developed in the United States.
And profit shifting remains a real problem.
That is what the Irish data from 2025 shows us. And
there is no sign that 2026 will be much different.
A lot of that is Apple and Microsoft.

Both have global profits will in excess of the total
profits of all the large American pharmaceutical
companies.

### Page 6

6
But Apple and Microsoft are essentially moving the
profit from their foreign sales to Ireland. They still
pay a decent amount of U.S. tax (as do Alphabet and
Nvidia, which both U.S.-shored their intellectual
property a few years back).****
The tax game of “Big Pharma” (the top six U.S.
pharmaceutical companies by revenue) is a bit
different. Most aggressively try to move the profit on
their U.S. sales out of the U.S., which generally has
required offshoring both their intellectual property

and the actual production of the active ingredients for
their most profitable drugs.
Back in 2023 and 2024, the top six U.S.
pharmaceutical companies didn’t set aside anything
to pay expected U.S. tax. They have pointed out (to
me, and to Senator Wyden) that they did make cash
payments to the U.S. Treasury, but those payments
were the settlement of the legacy tax liability on their

### Page 7

7
pre-2017 profit (don’t ask, it is complicated—the key
words are deferral and deemed repatriation).
In 2025, the major pharmaceutical companies did set
aside a few billion dollars to pay U.S. tax. Eli Lilly
doesn’t seem to be quite as aggressive in sweeping
profits out of the U.S. as its peers; it has consistently
reported setting aside a bit of money to pay expected
U.S. taxes on its global profit. Johnson and Johnson
which historically has swept all its profit out of the

United States, also had to set aside funds to pay U.S.
tax as part of some litigation related tax gymnastics.
But make no mistake, Big Pharma continues to
engage in aggressive forms of tax avoidance —and
still pays almost nothing in U.S. tax. See the chart
below.
And there is no evidence—at least, none that I see—
that suggests any of the Trump deals have changed

### Page 8

8
the core tax structure of big pharma.*****
The top six generally still report losing money on their
U.S. operations.
The big companies also generally report that the U.S.
accounts for most of their revenue.
And they generally report that they make most of
their money offshore (particularly if “special
situations” like corporate reorganizations, mergers,
and litigation set asides are left out). Just look at the
firms’ own 10-Ks.
AbbVie reports losing $3.5 billion in the U.S. and

making $10 billion abroad.
Pfizer reports just $776 million in U.S. profit and
almost $7 billion abroad.
Bristol Myers Squib reported a $19 million loss in the
U.S. and $9.3 billion profit abroad.
Merck reported a $5 billion loss in the U.S. and $26
billion profit abroad.
Johnson and Johnson would be the same in a typical
year. Back in 2024, it reported a $500 million loss in
the U.S. and $17 billion profit abroad, but its 2025
tax liability was driven by the reversal of some

litigation set asides (see page 69 of J&J’s latest 10-K).

### Page 9

9
Lilly seems to have a more balanced tax structure: in
2025, it reported making $14 billion in the U.S. and
$12 billion abroad.
As a result, the leading U.S. pharmaceutical
companies pay more in tax abroad than in the United
States, with several firms setting aside nothing to
cover their expected U.S. income tax liability.
In a normal year, without any corporate
reorganizations or litigation related profits, only one
of the top six would set aside a meaningful sum to pay

U.S. tax. So, in a typical year, there would something
like $100 billion in profits and only a few billion set
aside to pay expected U.S. tax.

### Page 10

10
This shows up in a couple of easily verifiable plots.
While Big Pharma is the most obvious case, the
incentive to offshore jobs, production, and profits isn’t
limited to one sector.
In the technology sector, for example, a number of
firms set aside much less to cover expected U.S.

### Page 11

11
corporate tax payments than would be expected given
their large global profits.
Take a very strategic sector, the production of the
equipment used to manufacture semiconductors.
Applied Materials reports making all its money
abroad (almost certainly in Singapore, as noted in
their 10-K, where Applied Materials “have been
granted additional conditional reduced tax rates”
which expire in fiscal 2030) and almost nothing in
the U.S.

### Page 12

12
Lam Research also reports making all its income
abroad, using Malaysia as its tax hub (from its 10-K:
“Effective from fiscal year 2022, the Company has a
15-year tax incentive ruling in Malaysia for one of its
foreign subsidiaries.”)
KLA is a bit more patriotic; it actually pays a bit of
U.S. tax.

### Page 13

13
To be sure, none of this is in any way illegal. Every
firm can honestly report that it pays all the tax that it
is legally due.
The issue is the law, which continues to create “pay
tax in America last” incentives, and often “produce in
America last” incentives too.
This can and should be changed, likely through a
bipartisan coalition that unites around a patriotic tax
agenda.
* Most U.S. big pharma firms are still paying a bit of
cash to the U.S. Treasury to settle their deferred

international tax liability from the ere Tax Cuts and
(Irish) Jobs Act; the cash tax paid thus exceeds the
amount they set aside out of current profit for
expected current tax. A few firms have tried to use
this fact to obscure the fact that they don’t think they
owe anything to the U.S. federal government out of
their 2023 or 2024 profits.
** Alphabet and Nvidia benefit from the lower tax
rate on foreign profits in the U.S. tax code; they aren’t

paying tax on their foreign profits at 21 percent. But
because their IP is in the U.S., the U.S. government
gets the lion’s share of their tax paid.   When the IP is
offshore, the U.S. only gets a small top up tax.
***

of at least €750 million are subject to a 15 percent
minimum effective tax rate under Pillar Two.

shifting.

## Document 306: Tokenised money and the future of FX (2026-09-10)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Tokenised money  and the future of FX (2026-09-10).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 28
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Tokenised money  
and the future of FX

1 
Authors 
Rohini Grover, Ph.D. 
Strategist

10 September 2026 
Tokenisation has the potential to transform the way financial markets operate, from the 
movement of money and assets to the management of liquidity and risk. In this paper, we 
focus on tokenised forms of money and their impact on FX markets. 
Many emerging use cases for tokenised money are driven by a desire to reduce frictions 
associated with cross-border payments and FX settlement. Stablecoins enable 24/7 
cross-border transfers but remain overwhelmingly US dollar-denominated. As a result, 
they are currently more relevant as digital dollar infrastructure and for bridge-currency 
arrangements, often creating a "stablecoin sandwich" in which the US dollar serves as an 
intermediary between two currency legs, rather than providing a complete solution to the 
two-currency FX settlement problem 
Tokenisation may play a bigger role in transforming the combined securities and FX 
settlement cycles involved in cross-border capital flows. This is an area of increasing 
complexity, as delays or failures in one part of the transaction chain can affect the timely 
completion of the wider transaction, increasing operational, liquidity, and settlement 
risks. For example, following the transition to T+1 securities settlement in North America 
and with Europe preparing to move to T+1, the need to coordinate FX, cash, and securities 
settlement across institutions, jurisdictions, and infrastructures is already increasing. 
Tokenisation enables programmability, which allows atomic settlement and a range of 
operational and control functions across financial market infrastructures, including real-
time liquidity management, margin management, collateral mobilisation and substitution, 
and automated compliance checks. Current implementations have focused primarily on 
supporting corporate treasury and liquidity management activities involving FX. However, 
the settlement of tokenised securities and the associated FX transactions may offer even 
greater efficiencies by enabling synchronised settlement and reducing some of the 
operational, funding, and coordination frictions associated with shorter settlement cycles. 
Interoperability between different currencies, tokenised money arrangements, and 
payment systems remains one of the key challenges facing the development of tokenised 
cross-border payments and FX settlement. Several initiatives are exploring solutions 
involving tokenised central bank reserves or hybrid models that combine tokenised 
commercial bank money with central bank money. These may be particularly relevant for 
multi-currency settlement frameworks, preserving the two-tier monetary system while 
supporting settlement finality and more integrated cross-border processes. In parallel, 
other initiatives are building infrastructure that enables different forms of money, payment 
systems, and settlement architectures to operate together. 
Tokenisation promises substantial benefits but unintended consequences are worth 
considering. Beyond faster settlement, wholesale FX markets also depend on liquidity 
efficiency. This means banks and other financial institutions can settle large transaction 
volumes safely while using less liquidity. Existing infrastructures such as Continuous 
Linked Settlement (CLS) combine settlement risk mitigation with significant liquidity 
savings through multilateral netting and liquidity optimisation mechanisms. Unlike CLS, 
most current tokenised settlement models do not yet provide multilateral netting at

### Page 2

Deutsche Bank Research Institute 
Iran Shock: Key transmission channels – March 3, 2026

2

comparable scale. A number of initiatives are exploring liquidity-saving mechanisms and 
new approaches to liquidity management under tokenisation. 
We expect a period of accelerating change in years ahead. Securities and currencies 
settlement involves a complex nexus of technology, market infrastructure, regulation, and 
legal frameworks, necessitating cooperation between both public and private sector 
participants across jurisdictions.

### Page 3

10 September 2026
FX Special Report
Deutsche Bank AG Page 3
